\Cref{thm:merged-riordan} & Riordan action, product, and inverse & partial & \lean{Fabius.expRiordan_action}, \lean{Fabius.expRiordan_mul}, \lean{Fabius.expRiordan_mul_inverse} (\lean{ExponentialRiordan}) prove the action, product, and a conditional one-sided inverse law, assuming the inverse series and $g\,(h\circ f)=1$.  Construction of these inverse series from the theorem's unit hypotheses and the full two-sided inverse statement remain to be formalized.  The Stirling examples are \lean{Fabius.expRiordan_one_exp_sub_one} and \lean{Fabius.expRiordan_one_log}. \\
\Cref{thm:merged-complementary-bell} & EGF, recurrence, and Dobiński-type series & Lean & \lean{Fabius.complementaryBell} (\lean{ComplementaryBell}); \cref{eq:merged-complementary-egf} is \lean{Fabius.exp_subst_neg_exp_sub_one}, \cref{eq:merged-complementary-recurrence} is \lean{Fabius.complementaryBell_succ}, and \cref{eq:merged-complementary-dobinski} is \lean{Fabius.complementaryBell_eq_exp_mul_tsum} (the series converges absolutely as the Poisson moment series of \lean{Fabius.tsum_pow_mul_pow_div_factorial}) \\
\Cref{cor:merged-bell-convolution-inverse} & Convolution inverse of the Bell sequence & Lean & \lean{Fabius.sum_choose_bell_mul_complementaryBell} (\lean{ComplementaryBell}), from the addition law \lean{Bell.complete_add} of complete Bell polynomials at weights $\pm1$ \\

\Cref{thm:merged-appell} & Appell, translation, and explicit formulas & Lean & Bernoulli: the derivative identity is Mathlib's \lean{Polynomial.derivative_bernoulli}, the translation formula is \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eval_add} (\lean{BernoulliAppell}) and the explicit formula \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-explicit} is the definition \lean{Polynomial.bernoulli_def}; Euler: \lean{Fabius.eulerPolynomial} (\lean{EulerPolynomials}, defined from $2/(\EulerE^t+1)$ as an Appell sequence), \lean{Fabius.derivative_eulerPolynomial} and \lean{Fabius.eulerPolynomial_eval_add}, both translation formulas being instances of the general Appell lemma \lean{Fabius.appell_eval_add} \\
\Cref{thm:merged-bernoulli-euler-basic} & Difference and reflection identities & Lean & Mathlib: the difference identity \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-difference} is \lean{Polynomial.bernoulli_eval_one_add}, the reflection \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-reflection} is \lean{Polynomial.bernoulli_eval_one_sub}, and $\beta_{2m+1}=0$ is \lean{bernoulli_eq_zero_of_odd}; the Euler difference identity \cref{eq:merged-euler-difference} is \lean{Fabius.eulerPolynomial_eval_add_one_add} (\lean{EulerPolynomials}), the Euler reflection \cref{eq:merged-euler-reflection} is \lean{Fabius.eulerPolynomial_eval_one_sub} and $\mathsf E_{2m+1}(1/2)=0$ is \lean{Fabius.eulerPolynomial_eval_half_odd} (\lean{EulerReflection}) \\
\Cref{thm:merged-faulhaber} & Faulhaber with an offset & Lean & \lean{Fabius.sum_range_add_pow_eq_bernoulli_sub} (\lean{FaulhaberOffset}), telescoping Mathlib's \lean{Polynomial.bernoulli_eval_one_add}; the case $x=0$ is also Mathlib's \lean{Polynomial.sum_range_pow_eq_bernoulli_sub} (and \lean{sum_range_pow}, \lean{sum_Ico_pow} in the Bernoulli-number form) \\
\Cref{thm:merged-alternating-sums} & Alternating power sums & Lean & \lean{Fabius.sum_neg_one_pow_mul_pow_eq_eulerPolynomial} (\lean{EulerPolynomials}), in the form $\sum_{j<N}(-1)^j(x+j)^p=\bigl(\mathsf E_p(x)-(-1)^N\mathsf E_p(x+N)\bigr)/2$, valid for all $N\ge0$, by induction from the difference identity \\
\Cref{thm:merged-raabe} & Raabe multiplication & Lean & \lean{Fabius.raabe} (\lean{RaabeMultiplication}), in the form $\sum_{r<q}\beta_n(x+r/q)=q\,(1/q)^n\beta_n(qx)$, proved from the generating functions in $(\RationalNumbers[x])[[t]]$ exactly as in the text; \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-half} is \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eval_half} \\
\Cref{thm:merged-bernoulli-stirling-touchard} & Bernoulli--Stirling formula and Touchard moments & partial & \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-stirling} is \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eq_sum_stirlingSecond} (\lean{BernoulliStirling}) for Mathlib's \lean{bernoulli} (with $B_1=-1/2$), from \lean{Fabius.bernoulliPowerSeries_eq_logDivSeries_subst}: the Bernoulli generating function is $\log(1+u)/u$ at $u=\EulerE^t-1$; the two integral representations are not formalized \\
\Cref{thm:merged-bernoulli-difference} & Bernoulli polynomials from finite differences & Lean & \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-newton-basis} is \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eq_sum_fwdDiff} (module \lean{BernoulliNewtonBasis}), an identity in $\mathbb Q[x]$ with $\Delta^kx^n$ written out as $\sum_j(-1)^{k-j}\binom kj(x+j)^n$; the truncated Gregory composition is \lean{Fabius.X_pow_dvd_bernoulliPowerSeries_sub_gregory}. \\
\Cref{cor:merged-bernoulli-stirling-second-proof} & Second proof of the Bernoulli--Stirling formula & Lean & \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eq_sum_fwdDiff_zero} (module \lean{BernoulliNewtonBasis}): $\beta_n=\sum_k\frac{(-1)^k}{k+1}\Delta^k0^n$, and with the surjection formula \lean{Fabius.factorial_mul_stirlingSecond_eq_sum} this is \lean{Fabius.bernoulli_eq_sum_stirlingSecond}. \\
\Cref{lem:merged-log-base-change} & Change of coefficient algebra & none &  \\
\Cref{thm:merged-norlund-calculus} & Appell, difference, and convolution calculus & partial & For natural orders $\alpha,\gamma\in\mathbb N$ (module \lean{NorlundPolynomials}, $\beta_n^{(a)}$ defined by \lean{Fabius.norlund}): \cref{eq:merged-norlund-appell} is \lean{Fabius.derivative_norlund_succ}, \cref{eq:merged-norlund-translation} is \lean{Fabius.norlund_eval_add}, \cref{eq:merged-norlund-difference} is \lean{Fabius.norlund_succ_eval_add_one_sub} and \cref{eq:merged-norlund-convolution} is \lean{Fabius.norlund_add_eval_add}. The arbitrary-order source in \lean{NorlundGeneralized}, including \lean{Fabius.generalizedNorlund_succ_eval_add_one_sub} and \lean{Fabius.generalizedNorlund_eval_add_one_sub}, awaits compilation; the row is not promoted on source review alone. \\
\Cref{thm:merged-norlund-bell-diagonal} & Bell-polynomial construction and the N\"orlund diagonal & partial & The three diagonal displays are formalized for natural orders and rational evaluation points (module \lean{NorlundDiagonal}): \cref{eq:merged-norlund-polynomial-diagonal} is \lean{Fabius.norlund_diagonal}, \cref{eq:merged-norlund-number-diagonal} is \lean{Fabius.norlund_eval_zero_diagonal} and \cref{eq:merged-norlund-diagonal} is \lean{Fabius.coeff_bernoulliPowerSeries_pow_succ}. The arbitrary-order Bell and multiplicity constructions in \lean{NorlundGeneralized} and \lean{BellCompletePartitions} await compilation. Transport of the polynomial diagonal to an arbitrary coefficient algebra remains open. \\
\Cref{thm:merged-genocchi} & Bernoulli--Euler--Genocchi relations & Lean & with \lean{Fabius.genocchi} defined by $2(1-2^n)\beta_n$, the generating function \cref{eq:merged-genocchi-egf} is \lean{Fabius.egf_genocchi_mul_exp_add_one} (\lean{GenocchiNumbers}), i.e. the first equality of \cref{eq:merged-genocchi}, and the second equality is \lean{Fabius.genocchi_succ_eq} (\lean{EulerPolynomials}); \lean{Fabius.genocchi_one}, \lean{Fabius.genocchi_two}, \lean{Fabius.genocchi_odd} give the initial values and the parity \\
\Cref{thm:merged-cauchy-polynomials} & Integral, Stirling, difference, addition, and reflection formulas & partial & Module \lean{CauchyPolynomials} defines $b_n$ by \cref{eq:merged-bernoulli-second-egf} (\lean{Fabius.cauchyPoly}): \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-derivative} is \lean{Fabius.derivative_cauchyPoly_succ}, \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-difference} is \lean{Fabius.cauchyPoly_succ_eval_add_one_sub}, \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-addition} is \lean{Fabius.cauchyPoly_eval_add}, \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-explicit} is \lean{Fabius.cauchyPoly_succ_eq} and \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-stirling-numbers} is \lean{Fabius.cauchyPoly_eval_zero}; the integral representation \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-integral} and the reflection \cref{eq:merged-cauchy-reflection} are not formalized. \\

\Cref{thm:merged-catalan-reflection} & Catalan formula & Lean & The Dyck-word count is Mathlib's \lean{DyckWord.card_dyckWord_semilength_eq_catalan}; $\CatalanNumber n=\binom{2n}{n}/(n+1)$ is \lean{catalan_eq_centralBinom_div} and the difference form is \lean{Fabius.catalan_succ_eq_choose_sub_choose} (module \lean{CatalanGeneratingFunction}). \\
\Cref{thm:merged-catalan-first-return} & First return & partial & $C=1+zC^2$ is \lean{Fabius.catalanSeries_eq} and its uniqueness among power series \lean{Fabius.eq_catalanSeries_of_eq_one_add_X_mul_sq} (module \lean{CatalanGeneratingFunction}); the square-root closed form is \lean{Fabius.sqrtOf_one_sub_four_X} (\lean{SquareRootSeries}), where $\sqrt{\cdot}$ is the formal square root \lean{Fabius.sqrtOf} of a series with constant term $1$, defined as $\exp(\tfrac12\log)$ and unique by \lean{Fabius.sqrt_unique}; the radius of convergence and the branch cut are analytic and are not formalized. \\
\Cref{thm:merged-narayana} & Narayana formula & partial & Module \lean{NarayanaNumbers} defines $N(n,k)$ by the division-free determinant $\binom nk\binom{n-1}{k-1}-\binom n{k-1}\binom{n-1}k$ over $\IntegerNumbers$ (\lean{Fabius.narayana}), which vanishes outside $1\le k\le n$ without a side condition: \cref{eq:merged-narayana} is \lean{Fabius.narayana_mul} in the cleared form $nN(n,k)=\binom nk\binom n{k-1}$, the symmetry is \lean{Fabius.narayana_symm} and the row sum $\sum_kN(n,k)=\CatalanNumber n$ is \lean{Fabius.sum_narayana}; the peak-counting interpretation and the bivariate generating function \cref{eq:merged-narayana-gf} are not formalized. \\

\Cref{thm:merged-crt-certificate} & Chinese-remainder certificate & Lean & \lean{Fabius.int_prod_dvd_of_pairwise_coprime} and \lean{Fabius.int_eq_zero_of_modEq_zero_of_natAbs_lt_prod} prove product divisibility and the stronger full-product zero bound; \lean{Fabius.int_eq_of_modEq_of_natAbs_sub_lt_prod}, \lean{Fabius.int_eq_of_modEq_of_natAbs_add_lt_prod}, and \lean{Fabius.int_eq_of_modEq_of_two_mul_natAbs_lt_prod} give the difference, sum, and symmetric half-product equality certificates for finite families of signed, pairwise coprime integer moduli (\lean{IntegerCRTCertificate}). \\
